It’s usually the board members or my staff, and they're saying I’m ruining the company. It sounds so clear, so real, that I actually answer them out loud before I even realize that no one else in the room heard it.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods of feeling unusually energized or needing much less sleep than usual?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't experienced anything like that. I sleep my normal amount, or at least I try to. I definitely don't feel unusually energized. Honestly, dealing with the stress of the company and hearing them talk about me all the time just makes me feel pretty drained.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these voices?\n\nPatient: Um, let me think... it's been about three months. Yeah, for the past three months I've been hearing them coming from the walls or through my phone.\n\nDoctor: Have you started any new medications or used any recreational drugs or other substances around the time these voices began?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I don't use any recreational drugs or anything like that. And I haven't started any new medications either. I mean, nothing has changed at all with what I take. It's just... no, nothing like that.\n\nDoctor: Have these experiences affected your work or your ability to function day to day?\n\nPatient: Yeah, they really have. My senior staff have kind of quietly taken over the work I used to handle myself. \n\nAnd it's not just at the office. At home, my husband has had to manage almost all the household responsibilities. I just end up leaving things unfinished, or I don't even start them at all.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any other odd experiences besides hearing voices, such as seeing things that aren't there?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't seen anything. It's really just the voices. I don't see things that aren't there or anything like that.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe the voices are actually real and coming from your coworkers or phone, or do you think they might be part of your mind?\n\nPatient: I... I really believe they're real. I mean, I hear them as clearly as I hear you talking to me right now. It doesn't feel like it's just something my mind is making up. They sound exactly like my coworkers, and it sounds like it's coming right through the phone or from down the hall. It sounds so clear, so real, that I actually answer them out loud before I even realize that no one else in the room heard it.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods of feeling unusually energized or needing much less sleep than usual?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't experienced anything like that. I sleep my normal amount, or at least I try to. I definitely don't feel unusually energized. Honestly, dealing with the stress of the company and hearing them talk about me all the time just makes me feel pretty drained.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these voices?\n\nPatient: Um, let me think... it's been about three months. Yeah, for the past three months I've been hearing them coming from the walls or through my phone.\n\nDoctor: Have you started any new medications or used any recreational drugs or other substances around the time these voices began?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I don't use any recreational drugs or anything like that. And I haven't started any new medications either. I mean, nothing has changed at all with what I take. It's just... no, nothing like that.\n\nDoctor: Have these experiences affected your work or your ability to function day to day?\n\nPatient: Yeah, they really have. It's... it's hard to admit, but I'm just not reliably able to run the division right now. I can't seem to keep up with basic executive decisions anymore. My senior staff have kind of quietly taken over the work I used to handle myself. \n\nAnd it's not just at the office. At home, my husband has had to manage almost all the household responsibilities. I just end up leaving things unfinished, or I don't even start them at all.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any other odd experiences besides hearing voices, such as seeing things that aren't there?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't seen anything. It's really just the voices. I don't see things that aren't there or anything like that.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe the voices are actually real and coming from your coworkers or phone, or do you think they might be part of your mind?\n\nPatient: I... I really believe they're real. I mean, I hear them as clearly as I hear you talking to me right now. It doesn't feel like it's just something my mind is making up. They sound exactly like my coworkers, and it sounds like it's coming right through the phone or from down the hall. So... yeah, to me, they are absolutely real.\n\nDoctor: Besides the voices, have you had any other fixed beliefs that other people think are false or unreasonable?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, the voices are the main thing. I haven't really had any other... beliefs or ideas that people tell me are wrong. Just the voices from my coworkers.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling emotionally flat or detached most of the time?\n\nPatient: Yes, I suppose I have. I used to be the person driving every idea at the company, calling people right after openings, just really engaged in everything. But now... I can just sit at my desk for hours, staring at the agenda. I barely speak to my staff anymore, or even my husband at home. I just feel almost nothing about the work I used to love so much.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voices are caused by a real external source, or do you recognize them as part of your own mind?\n\nPatient: I... I think I just answered this a minute ago. But, um, yes, I believe they are coming from a real external source. Like I said, it's my coworkers. I don't think it's just my own mind making things up... they sound completely real to me, so I believe they are really there.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself becoming confused, hard to follow, or having trouble organizing your thoughts when you speak or write?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything like that. I mean, my thoughts feel clear enough to me. I don't think I'm confused or having trouble putting sentences together. Nobody has mentioned that I'm hard to follow, either.\n\nDoctor: Have the voices been happening continuously for at least six months?\n\nPatient: No, it hasn't been that long. The voices... the ones coming from the walls and my phone, they started about three months ago.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods of feeling unusually sad, empty, or depressed during the time these voices have been happening?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't really felt sad or depressed. I mean, it's stressful dealing with what my coworkers are doing, but I don't feel empty or down in that way. I just feel almost nothing about the work I used to love so much.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voices are caused by a real external source, or do you recognize them as part of your own mind?\n\nPatient: I... I think I just answered this a minute ago. But, um, yes, I believe they are coming from a real external source. Like I said, it's my coworkers. I don't think it's just my own mind making things up... they sound completely real to me, so I believe they are really there.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself becoming confused, hard to follow, or having trouble organizing your thoughts when you speak or write?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything like that. I mean, my thoughts feel clear enough to me. I don't think I'm confused or having trouble putting sentences together. Nobody has mentioned that I'm hard to follow, either.\n\nDoctor: Have the voices been happening continuously for at least six months?\n\nPatient: No, it hasn't been that long. The voices... the ones coming from the walls and my phone, they started about three months ago.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods of feeling unusually sad, empty, or depressed during the time these voices have been happening?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't really felt sad or depressed. I mean, it's stressful dealing with what my coworkers are doing, but I don't feel empty or down in that way. I'm just trying to figure out how to handle the situation.\n\nDoctor: How old were you when these voices first started?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm 43 now. And like I said, they just started about three months ago, so... I was 43.\n\nDoctor: Have you had the belief that your coworkers or phone are really speaking about you for at least one month?\n\nPatient: Yes. I mean, like I said, it all started about three months ago. So... yes, I've known they were talking about me for definitely longer than a month. It's been happening this whole time.\n\nDoctor: During the past three months, have you had any times when your mood was unusually elevated or irritable, with much more energy than usual?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I haven't felt overly energetic or, you know, high-strung like that. I've just been focused on trying to get my work done and deal with... well, with what's going on at the office. But my mood itself hasn't been unusually high or anything.\n\nDoctor: Apart from the voices, have you had any fixed beliefs that you strongly hold even if others say they’re not true?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I'm just trying to run my company and figure out why this is happening.\n\nDoctor: Have other people told you that your speech has become hard to follow or disorganized?\n\nPatient: No, nobody has said that to me.
